data.\\n/.upstream/\\n/.bun-cache/\\n/.task-tmp/\\n/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-07-pstack-openai/task-*-fixtures/\\n\\n# Preserve provenance, evidence and logs as reviewable package material.\\nMIT IN NO EVENT SHALL THE\\nAUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER\\nLIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,\\nOUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN THE\\nSOFTWARE.\\n# pstack portable package\\n\\nAn unofficial port of Lauren Tan's pstack v0.15.15. The pinned baseline includes 51 root skills, 24 principles, 23 playbooks and 164 source files. Read [UPSTREAM.md](UPSTREAM.md), [NOTICE.md](NOTICE.md) and [LICENSE](LICENSE) for lineage and attribution.\\n\\nThe implementation is a candidate awaiting aggregate review, frozen-package discovery and fresh host observations. Both hosts remain unverified. A local file read does not prove installed discovery. See [compatibility](docs/compatibility.md) before relying on a workflow.\\n\\n## Start here\\n\\n- [Personal ChatGPT setup and blocked route](docs/setup/chatgpt.md).\\n- [Codex setup and unverified documented route](docs/setup/codex.md).\\n- [Choose a workflow](docs/workflows/choosing-a-workflow.md).\\n- [All 51 skills](docs/skills/README.md) and [all 24 principles](docs/principles/README.md).\\n- [Preserved guide](docs/guide/README.md).\\n- [Arena walkthrough](docs/workflows/arena.md), [Swarm walkthrough](docs/workflows/swarm.md) and [Poteto mode walkthrough](docs/workflows/poteto-mode.md).\\n- [Troubleshooting](docs/troubleshooting.md) and [host test matrix](docs/testing/host-matrix.md).\\n- [Helper dependency setup](docs/testing/helper-setup.md) and [optional Benny readiness](docs/testing/benny-readiness.md).\\n- [Upstream check, prepare, apply, validation and separate acceptance](docs/maintenance/upstream-updates.md).\\n- [Baseline gate and personal customization boundary](docs/maintenance/customization-boundary.md).\\n- [Conflict resolution format](docs/maintenance/update-resolutions.example.json) and [rollback](docs/maintenance/rollback.md).\\n- [Package validator](tools/validate_package.py), [evidence validator](tools/validate_evidence.py), [update CLI](tools/sync_upstream.py), [release report CLI](tools/release_report.py) and [catalog renderer](tools/build_catalog.py).\\n- [Acceptance cases](docs/parity/cases/documentation-walkthroughs.json), [inventory](docs/parity/inventory.json) and [case registry](docs/parity/case-registry.json).\\n- [Design](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-pstack-openai-design.md) and [implementation plan](docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-07-pstack-openai.md).\\n\\n## Permissions and package identity\\n\\nInstructions grant no installation, settings, integration, external write or recurring schedule permission. Development dependencies and host plugin loading are separate approvals. Models and workers require current exposed operations and actual observations.\\n\\nCandidate navigation is frozen with operative content. The final handoff must keep these bytes unchanged or explicitly refreeze and relink fresh blocked observations. Evidence/report commits can follow the tested commit only under the exact [tested-package marker contract](docs/maintenance/rollback.md). After blocked host records enter the inventory and regenerate the catalog, an explicit second freeze is required. Fresh blocked discovery records must then name that final SHA. Tests verify its 164 paths and recorded per-file hashes against the pinned source; they never fetch or execute it. A missing fixture is an explicit test prerequisite failure. Run maintenance tests through Bun with Python bytecode disabled:\\n\\n```sh\\nPSTACK_SOURCE_FIXTURE=/absolute/path/to/pinned/pstack bun --no-install -e 'const r=Bun.spawnSync([\\\"python3\\\",\\\"-B\\\",\\\"-m\\\",\\\"unittest\\\",\\\"discover\\\",\\\"-s\\\",\\\"tests\\\",\\\"-v\\\"],{env:{...process.env,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE:\\\"1\\\"},stdout:\\\"inherit\\\",stderr:\\\"inherit\\\"});process.exit(r.exitCode)'\\n```\\n{\\n  \\\"name\\\": \\\"@cursor-skill/poteto-mode-tools\\\",\\n  \\\"private\\\": true,\\n  \\\"type\\\": \\\"module\\\",\\n  \\\"scripts\\\": {\\n    \\\"test\\\": \\\"bun test orch watch-pr\\\",\\n    \\\"typecheck\\\": \\\"tsc --project watch-pr/tsconfig.json --noEmit --strict\\\"\\n  },\\n  \\\"dependencies\\\": {\\n    \\\"commander\\\": \\\"14.0.0\\\"\\n  },\\n  \\\"devDependencies\\\": {\\n    \\\"bun-types\\\": \\\"1.3.14\\\",\\n    \\\"typescript\\\": \\\"7.0.2\\\"\\n  }\\n}\\nimport { readFileSync } from \\\"node:fs\\\";\\nimport { join } from \\\"node:path\\\";\\n\\nconst pins = { commander: \\\"14.0.0\\\", \\\"bun-types\\\": \\\"1.3.14\\\", typescript: \\\"7.0.2\\\" } as const;\\n\\n// Keep the caller API, but setup is always an explicit operator action.\\nexport function ensureDependenciesInstalled(): void {\\n  try {\\n    const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(import.meta.dir, \\\"package.json\\\"), \\\"utf8\\\"));\\n    // Bun's text lockfile is JSON with trailing commas.\\n    const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(import.meta.dir, \\\"bun.lock\\\"), \\\"utf8\\\").replace(/,\\\\s*([}\\\\]])/g, \\\"$1\\\"));\\n    for (const [name, version] of Object.entries(pins)) {\\n      const requested = manifest.dependencies?.[name] ?? manifest.devDependencies?.[name];\\n      if (requested !== version) throw new Error(`${name} must be pinned to ${version}`);\\n      if (lock.packages?.[name]?.[0] !== `${name}@${version}`) throw new Error(`${name} lock resolution must be ${version}`);\\n      const installed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(import.meta.dir, \\\"node_modules\\\", name, \\\"package.json\\\"), \\\"utf8\\\"));\\n      if (installed.name !== name || installed.version !== version) throw new Error(`${name} installed version must be ${version}`);\\n    }\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    throw new Error(\\\"Helper setup required: prepare a writable dependency copy using docs/testing/helper-setup.md. \\\" + String(error));\\n  }\\n}\\n{\\n  \\\"discoveryOnly\\\": true,\\n  \\\"compilerExecuted\\\": false,\\n  \\\"hostProof\\\": false,\\n  \\\"paths\\\": [\\n    \\\"/Users/digital-hawk/.bun/install/cache/typescript@5.9.3@@@1/lib/tsc.js\\\",\\n    \\\"/Users/digital-hawk/.bun/install/global/node_modules/typescript/lib/tsc.js\\\",\\n    \\\"/Users/digital-hawk/.codex/plugins/cache/yabasha-local/pstack-openai/0.2.1/skills/poteto-mode/scripts/node_modules/typescript/lib/tsc.js\\\"\\n  ]\\n}\\n\"},\"logo\":\"Actual view_image rendering: 512x512 anime-style smiling woman with dark hair and glasses eating a purple-skinned yellow sweet potato, pale blue background.
